P(G, X) =
r=1
P(G = k|X = x) =
r=1 πrPr(G = k)φ(x; µr, Σ)
,
P(X|G = k) =
r=1 πrPr(G = k)φ(x; µr, Σ)
r=1 πrPr(G = k)
,
where πrk = πrPr(G = k)/ PR
